```
{'unfold1d_tensor': 'unfold a TxBxC PyTorch tensor to TxBxCxK using a specified kernel size and left padding', 'unfold1d_with_padding': 'unfold a 3D tensor with custom left padding and pad value for sequence windowing', 'unfold1d_kernel_one': 'unfold a tensor with kernel size 1 to add a trailing dimension via unsqueeze', 'review_unfold1d_strided': 'review the unfold1d function that uses as_strided to create sliding window views of a tensor', 'summarize_unfold1d': 'summarize the unfold1d function that pads and strides a TxBxC tensor into TxBxCxK shape'}
```
